#### 6. Arabic Lots

**God View: B — A 5, B 13**
- Detail: 18 source-tagged lots are calculated once per chart and scored by house side.
- Formula: Σ lot score by house side; angular houses ×2; Nemesis is negative
- Source: current-seven-layer-territorial
**Inputs / geometry:**
- Lot of Fortune: Asc + Moon − Sun | night: Asc + Sun − Moon; 157.86° Virgo, H6, A, score 1; source Paulus Alexandrinus, Introduction, Hermetic Lots
- Lot of Spirit: Asc + Sun − Moon | night: Asc + Moon − Sun; 202.14° Libra, H7, B, score 2; source Paulus Alexandrinus, Introduction, Hermetic Lots
- Lot of Eros: Asc + Venus − Spirit | night: Asc + Spirit − Venus; 356.26° Pisces, H12, B, score 1; source Paulus Alexandrinus, Introduction, Hermetic Lots
- Lot of Victory: Asc + Jupiter − Spirit | night: Asc + Spirit − Jupiter; 193.27° Libra, H7, B, score 2; source Paulus Alexandrinus, Introduction, Hermetic Lots
- Lot of Necessity: Asc + Fortune − Mercury | night: Asc + Mercury − Fortune; 256.62° Sagittarius, H9, B, score 1; source Paulus Alexandrinus, Introduction, Hermetic Lots
- Lot of Courage: Asc + Fortune − Mars | night: Asc + Mars − Fortune; 279.47° Capricorn, H10, A, score 2; source Paulus Alexandrinus, Introduction, Hermetic Lots
- Lot of Nemesis: Asc + Fortune − Saturn | night: Asc + Saturn − Fortune; 184.60° Libra, H7, B, score -2; source Paulus Alexandrinus, Introduction, Hermetic Lots
- Lot of Basis: Asc + Spirit − Fortune | night: Asc + Fortune − Spirit; 44.29° Taurus, H2, A, score 1; source Vettius Valens, Anthology, principal lots
- Lot of Exaltation: Asc + 18° Aries − Sun | night: Asc + 2° Taurus − Moon; 137.22° Leo, H5, B, score 1; source Vettius Valens, Hermetic/Exaltation lot tradition
- Lot of Action: Asc + Mars − Mercury | night: Asc + Mercury − Mars; 337.15° Pisces, H12, B, score 1; source Rhetorius the Egyptian, Lot of Activity / Work tradition
- Lot of Death: Saturn + 8th-house cusp − Moon | night: Saturn + 8th-house cusp − Moon; 144.62° Leo, H5, B, score 1; source Dorotheus of Sidon, Carmen Astrologicum, Death lot
- Lot of Property: Asc + Jupiter − Mercury | night: Asc + Mercury − Jupiter; 134.18° Leo, H5, B, score 1; source Olympiodorus, Property Disposition lot
- Lot of Travel: Asc + Mars − Sun | night: Asc + Sun − Mars; 357.61° Pisces, H12, B, score 1; source Firmicus Maternus, Mathesis, Travel lot
- Lot of Fame: Asc + Venus − Jupiter | night: Asc + Jupiter − Venus; 162.98° Virgo, H6, A, score 1; source Olympiodorus, Fame / Glory lot
- Lot of Enemies: Asc + Mars − Saturn | night: Asc + Saturn − Mars; 265.13° Sagittarius, H9, B, score 1; source Ancients / Olympiodorus, Enemies lot
- Lot of Success: Asc + Jupiter − Fortune | night: Asc + Fortune − Jupiter; 237.56° Scorpio, H8, B, score 1; source Abu Ma'shar, Great Introduction, success tradition
- Lot of Triumph: Asc + Venus − Saturn | night: Asc + Saturn − Venus; 225.14° Scorpio, H8, B, score 1; source Hermetic extension, Recovered sports lot set
- Lot of Glory: Asc + Sun − Saturn | night: Asc + Saturn − Sun; 267.52° Sagittarius, H9, B, score 1; source Hermetic extension, Recovered sports lot set
**Calculation steps:**
- Lot of Fortune → A: +1; Reverse the luminary arc at night.
- Lot of Spirit → B: +2; Reverse the luminary arc at night.
- Lot of Eros → B: +1; Reverse Venus ↔ Spirit at night.
- Lot of Victory → B: +2; Reverse Jupiter ↔ Spirit at night.
- Lot of Necessity → B: +1; Reverse Fortune ↔ Mercury at night.
- Lot of Courage → A: +2; Reverse Fortune ↔ Mars at night.
- Lot of Nemesis → B: -2; Reverse Fortune ↔ Saturn at night; score is negative.
- Lot of Basis → A: +1; Reverse Spirit ↔ Fortune at night.
- Lot of Exaltation → B: +1; Use the day fixed point 18° Aries or night fixed point 2° Taurus.
- Lot of Action → B: +1; Reverse Mars ↔ Mercury at night.
- Lot of Death → B: +1; No day/night reversal in the selected Dorothean variant.
- Lot of Property → B: +1; Reverse Jupiter ↔ Mercury at night.
- Lot of Travel → B: +1; Reverse Mars ↔ Sun at night.
- Lot of Fame → A: +1; Reverse Venus ↔ Jupiter at night.
- Lot of Enemies → B: +1; Reverse Mars ↔ Saturn at night.
- Lot of Success → B: +1; Reverse Jupiter ↔ Fortune at night.
- Lot of Triumph → B: +1; Reverse Venus ↔ Saturn at night.
- Lot of Glory → B: +1; Reverse Sun ↔ Saturn at night.
- Final method output: A 5.000 vs B 13.000 → Side B.
- Hit/miss test: MISS because method call B does not match verified result A.

## Audit notes

- This documents every engine-exposed aspect, planetary/house input and formula for all 21 methods in both original frames, not every theoretical planet-to-planet contact outside the engine's aspect filters.
- Cross-side contacts in HFU may be recorded without directional points. This remains a method-design limitation.
- Moon VOC is not fully computed from future applying aspects before the next sign boundary.
- Numerical blending does not make the Synthesis statistically independent from God/Agent.
- The sample is seven historical games, not the requested all-rivalries multi-decade census.
- Kickoff and odds provenance need additional verification before accuracy claims.
